{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0.59 L 7.87 0.59}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
{}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 0 8.46}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}_{\mathmakebox[0pt][r]{\mathbb{R}}}}{\mathop{}\kern 5.3473pt\mathopen{\vphantom{\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to6.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-0.4pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0.59 L 7.87 0.59}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
{}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 0 8.46}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}_{\mathmakebox[0pt][r]{\mathbb{R}}}}\hbox to6.49pt{\vbox to6.92pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.4pt\lower-0.4pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0.59 L 7.87 0.59}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
{}{{}}{}
{}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 0 8.46}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{q}

given by

\iota^{pq}(i,j)_{p+q}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha+\delta=i\\
\beta+\gamma=j\end{subarray}}(-1)^{(\alpha+\beta)(\delta+\gamma+q)}(\alpha,\beta)_{p}\otimes(\delta,\gamma)_{q}

are maps of
\mathbb{R}
-bicomplexes. That is,

\sigma\iota^{pq}\sigma=\iota^{pq}.

This follows from the observation that if
i+j=p+q
, then the action of
\sigma
does not introduce any signs to either
(i,j)_{p+q}
or the summands
(\alpha,\beta)_{p}\otimes(\delta,\gamma)_{q}
of
\iota^{pq}(i,j)_{p+q}
(see equation (
6
)). And if
i+j=p+q-1
, then for each summand
(\alpha,\beta)_{p}\otimes(\delta,\gamma)_{q}
, either
\alpha+\beta=p-1
or
\delta+\gamma=q-1
, but not both. Then, the action of
\sigma
on
(i,j)_{p+q}
and on each summand introduces a minus sign.
∎

As a consequence of the previous proposition,
\mathcal{B}_{\mathbb{R}}
is a lax monoidal functor when restricted to third quadrant bicomplexes. Thus, we may endow
\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}
with a dg-structure by defining the Hom-objects for
A,B\in\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}
as

[A,B]=\mathcal{E}_{\mathbb{R}}(\underline{\mathrm{Hom}}_{\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}}(A,B)),

where
\mathcal{E}_{\mathbb{R}}
is the composition of functors

\mathcal{E}_{\mathbb{R}}:\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}\xrightarrow{\tau_{\mathord{\mspace{1mu}\text{\hskip 1.07639pt\hbox to2.45pt{\vbox to2.45pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.15pt\lower-0.15pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@roundcap\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 2.98 0 L 2.98 2.98 L 0 2.98 M 1.64 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mspace{1mu}}}}\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathord{\mspace{1mu}\text{\hskip 1.50694pt\hbox to3.31pt{\vbox to3.31pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.15pt\lower-0.15pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@roundcap\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 4.17 0 L 4.17 4.17 L 0 4.17 M 2.29 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mspace{1mu}}}\xrightarrow{\mathcal{B}_{\mathbb{R}}}\mathrm{Ch}_{\mathbb{R}}^{\leq 0}

and
\tau_{\mathord{\mspace{1mu}\text{\hskip 1.50694pt\hbox to3.31pt{\vbox to3.31pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 0.15pt\lower-0.15pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@roundcap\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-linecap} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{{}{{}}{}{}{}{}{}{{}}{}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 4.17 0 L 4.17 4.17 L 0 4.17 M 2.29 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}\mspace{1mu}}}
is defined as in (
5
). Denote the resulting dg-category by
\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathrm{Ch}}
.

Similarly, we may obtain a simplicial category
\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathrm{sSet}}
whose Hom-objects are given by

[A,B]=U\Gamma\mathcal{E}_{\mathbb{R}}(\underline{\mathrm{Hom}}_{\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}}(A,B)),

where
U\Gamma
is the composition functor

\mathrm{Ch}^{\leq 0}_{\mathbb{R}}\xrightarrow{\Gamma}s\mathrm{Mod}_{\mathbb{R}}\xrightarrow{U}\mathrm{sSet}.

given by the Dold-Kan correspondence and the forgetful functor.

As before, denote by
\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}[W^{-1}]
the
\infty
-category given by the Dwyer-Kan localization with respect to pluripotential weak equivalences. Precisely the same arguments as in Theorem
5.1
prove the theorem below.

Theorem 6.8
.

There is a chain of equivalences of
\infty
-categories

\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}[W^{-1}]\simeq N_{hc}(\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathrm{sSet}})\simeq N_{dg}(\mathbb{R}\mathrm{BiCo}_{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathrm{Ch}}).
